# Actions, resources, and condition keys for AWS Glue

AWS Glue (service prefix: `glue`) provides the following
service-specific operations, resources, actions, and condition keys for use in IAM permission
policies.

References:

- Learn how to [configure this service](../../../glue/latest/dg.md "../../../glue/latest/dg.md").
- View a list of the [API operations available for
  this service](../../../glue/latest/dg/aws-glue-api.md "../../../glue/latest/dg/aws-glue-api.md").
- Learn how to secure this service and its resources by
  [using IAM](../../../glue/latest/dg/authentication-and-access-control.md "../../../glue/latest/dg/authentication-and-access-control.md") permission policies.
- View the [programmatic service authorization
  reference](https://servicereference.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/v1/glue/glue.json "https://servicereference.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/v1/glue/glue.json") for this service.

###### Topics

- [API operations defined by AWS Glue](#list_glue-operations "#list_glue-operations")
- [Actions defined by AWS Glue](#list_glue-actions-as-permissions "#list_glue-actions-as-permissions")
- [Permission-only actions for AWS Glue](#list_glue-permission-only-actions "#list_glue-permission-only-actions")
- [Resource types defined by AWS Glue](#list_glue-resources-for-iam-policies "#list_glue-resources-for-iam-policies")
- [Condition keys for AWS Glue](#list_glue-policy-keys "#list_glue-policy-keys")

## API operations defined by AWS Glue

The following table maps API operations to the IAM actions they authorize. ## Actions defined by AWS Glue

You can specify the following actions in the `Action` element of an IAM
policy statement. Use policies to grant permissions to perform an operation in AWS. When
you use an action in a policy, you usually allow or deny access to the API operation or CLI
command with the same name. However, in some cases, a single action controls access to more
than one operation. Alternatively, some operations require several different actions.

| Actions                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Resource types (\*required)                                                                             | Condition keys                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Access level                  |

## Permission-only actions for AWS Glue

The following actions are defined by AWS Glue but are not directly
invocable through any API operation. They can only be used in IAM policy statements
to grant or deny permissions.

| Actions                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Description                                                                                                                          | Resource types (\*required)                                                                             | Condition keys                                                                                           | Access level |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ |
| [AuthorizeInboundIntegration](aws-glue-api-integrations.md "aws-glue-api-integrations.md")                                                                                                                                                      | Grants permission to Glue to continuously validate that the target Arn can receive data replicated from the source ARN               | [integration\*](#list_glue-resource-integration "#list_glue-resource-integration")                      | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_") | Write        |
| [CreateInboundIntegration](aws-glue-api-integrations.md "aws-glue-api-integrations.md")                                                                                                                                                         | Grants permission to the source principal to create an inbound integration for data to be replicated from the source into the target |                                                                                                         |                                                                                                          | Write        |
| [PassConnection](../../../glue/latest/dg/aws-glue-api-catalog-connections.md#aws-glue-api-catalog-connections-CreateConnection "../../../glue/latest/dg/aws-glue-api-catalog-connections.md#aws-glue-api-catalog-connections-CreateConnection") | Grants permission to pass glue connection name in input for APIs that require them                                                   | [connection\*](#list_glue-resource-connection "#list_glue-resource-connection")                         | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_") | Write        |
| [PublishDataQuality](../../../glue/latest/dg/aws-glue-api-data-quality-api.md "../../../glue/latest/dg/aws-glue-api-data-quality-api.md")                                                                                                       | Grants permission to publish Data Quality results                                                                                    | [dataQualityRuleset\*](#list_glue-resource-dataQualityRuleset "#list_glue-resource-dataQualityRuleset") | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_") | Write        |
| [UseMLTransforms](../../../glue/latest/dg/aws-glue-api-machine-learning-api.md "../../../glue/latest/dg/aws-glue-api-machine-learning-api.md")                                                                                                  | Grants permission to use an ML Transform from within a Glue ETL Script                                                               | [mlTransform\*](#list_glue-resource-mlTransform "#list_glue-resource-mlTransform")                      | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_") | Write        |

| [userdefinedfunction](../../../glue/latest/dg/glue-specifying-resource-arns.md "../../../glue/latest/dg/glue-specifying-resource-arns.md")         | arn:${Partition}:glue:${Region}:${Account}:userDefinedFunction/${DatabaseName}/${UserDefinedFunctionName} | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_") |
| [workflow](../../../glue/latest/dg/glue-specifying-resource-arns.md "../../../glue/latest/dg/glue-specifying-resource-arns.md")                    | arn:${Partition}:glue:${Region}:${Account}:workflow/${WorkflowName}                                       | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_glue-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_") |

## Condition keys for AWS Glue

AWS Glue defines the following condition keys that can be used in the
`Condition` element of an IAM policy.

| Condition keys                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Description                                                                                                           | Type          |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------- |
| [aws:RequestTag/${TagKey}](../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-requesttag "../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-requesttag")                         | Filters access by the presence of tag key-value pairs in the request                                                  | String        |
| [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-resourcetag "../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-resourcetag")                      | Filters access by tag key-value pairs attached to the resource                                                        | String        |
| [aws:TagKeys](../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-tagkeys "../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-tagkeys")                                            | Filters access by the presence of tag keys in the request                                                             | ArrayOfString |
| [glue:CredentialIssuingService](../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys "../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys")        | Filters access by the service from which the credentials of the request is issued                                     | String        |
| [glue:EnabledForRedshiftAutoDiscovery](../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys "../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys") | Filters access by the presence of the key configured for role's identity-based policy                                 | Bool          |
| [glue:FederatedAuthorizationSource](../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys "../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys")    | Filters access by whether the resource belongs to federated authorization                                             | String        |
| [glue:LakeFormationPermissions](../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys "../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys")        | Filters access by whether Lake Formation permission checks will be performed for a given caller and the Glue resource | String        |
| [glue:RoleAssumedBy](../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys "../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys")                   | Filters access by the service from which the credentials of the request is obtained by assuming the customer role     | String        |
| [glue:SecurityGroupIds](../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys "../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys")                | Filters access by the ID of security groups configured for the Glue job                                               | ArrayOfString |
| [glue:SubnetIds](../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys "../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys")                       | Filters access by the ID of subnets configured for the Glue job                                                       | ArrayOfString |
| [glue:VpcIds](../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys "../../../glue/latest/dg/using-identity-based-policies.md#glue-identity-based-policy-condition-keys")                          | Filters access by the ID of the VPC configured for the Glue job                                                       | ArrayOfString |
